Alert Detail

Medium
 Content Security Policy (CSP) Header Not Set
Description
Content Security Policy (CSP) is an added layer of security that helps to detect and mitigate certain types of attacks, including Cross Site Scripting (XSS) and data injection attacks. These attacks are used for everything from data theft to site defacement or distribution of malware. CSP provides a set of standard HTTP headers that allow website owners to declare approved sources of content that browsers should be allowed to load on that page — covered types are JavaScript, CSS, HTML frames, fonts, images and embeddable objects such as Java applets, ActiveX, audio and video files.

Method GET
Parameter
Attack
Evidence
Other Info
Instances 1
Solution
Ensure that your web server, application server, load balancer, etc. is configured to set the Content-Security-Policy header.

Medium
 Cross-Domain Misconfiguration
Description
Web browser data loading may be possible, due to a Cross Origin Resource Sharing (CORS) misconfiguration on the web server.
URL https://gestion-leads-sociedad.vercel.app/
Node Name https://gestion-leads-sociedad.vercel.app/
Method GET
Parameter
Attack
Evidence Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *
Other Info
The CORS misconfiguration on the web server permits cross-domain read requests from arbitrary third party domains, using unauthenticated APIs on this domain. Web browser implementations do not permit arbitrary third parties to read the response from authenticated APIs, however. This reduces the risk somewhat. This misconfiguration could be used by an attacker to access data that is available in an unauthenticated manner, but which uses some other form of security, such as IP address white-listing.
Instances 1
Solution
Ensure that sensitive data is not available in an unauthenticated manner (using IP address white-listing, for instance).

Configure the "Access-Control-Allow-Origin" HTTP header to a more restrictive set of domains, or remove all CORS headers entirely, to allow the web browser to enforce the Same Origin Policy (SOP) in a more restrictive manner.
Reference https://vulncat.fortify.com/en/detail?category=HTML5&subcategory=Overly%20Permissive%20CORS%20Policy

border-slate-700 rounded-xl p-4 font-mono text-[11px] text-emerald-400 overflow-x-auto select-all leading-relaxed"> -- ======================================================= -- 1. CREACIÓN DE LA TABLA 'leads' (CON RESPETO A COLUMNAS) -- ======================================================= CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.leads ( id BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, "Mes" TEXT, "Fecha" TEXT, "Nombre" TEXT NOT NULL, "Telefono" TEXT, "Campaña" TEXT, "Medio" TEXT, "GESTION" TEXT DEFAULT 'Información ', "Interesado en " TEXT, "Ciudad" TEXT, "AGENTE" TEXT, "Odoo" TEXT, "Fecha de Atencion" TEXT, "OBSERVACIONES " TEXT, "Fecha Última Gestión " TEXT, "ULTIMA GESTION" TEXT, "ULTIMO AGENTE " TEXT, "LANDING" TEXT, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(), updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); -- ======================================================= -- 2. HABILITACIÓN DE ROW LEVEL SECURITY (RLS) -- ======================================================= ALTER TABLE public.leads ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY; -- ======================================================= -- 3. POLÍTICAS DE ACCESO ESTRICTAS (POLICIES) -- ======================================================= -- La política segura se instala mediante la migración versionada. -- Las inserciones y actualizaciones se ejecutan mediante RPCs estrechas. -- No existe política directa de escritura para el navegador. -- No existe política DELETE: el archivado se hace por RPC admin. -- ======================================================= -- 4. HABILITACIÓN DE SINCRONIZACIÓN EN TIEMPO REAL -- ======================================================= ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.leads; </pre> <button onclick="copyRlsCode()" class="absolute top-3 right-3 px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-xs text-slate-200 border border-slate-700 flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"> <i data-lucide="copy" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i> <span id="copyRlsBtnText">Copiar SQL</span> </button> </div> <div class="p-3 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700/80 rounded-xl space-y-1 text-slate-300"> <div class="font-semibold text-white flex items-center gap-1.5"> <i data-lucide="info" class="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-400"></i> <span>Credenciales en uso</span> </div> <p class="font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate">URL: https://hkkuyomlcqyxtzblowle.supabase.co</p> <p class="font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate">La clave anon se usa solo en el navegador; las operaciones sensibles requieren sesión y RPC.</p> </div> </div> <div class="px-6 py-3 border-t border-slate-700/80 bg-slate-900/50 flex justify-end shrink-0"> <button onclick="closeRlsModal()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-600 text-slate-200 cursor-pointer">Entendido</button> </div> </div> </div> <!-- ================= TOAST NOTIFICATION CONTAINER ================= --> <div id="toastContainer" class="fixed bottom-4 right-4 z-50 flex flex-col gap-2 pointer-events-none"></div> <!-- ================= LÓGICA DE JAVASCRIPT (CORREGIDA PARA LEER SUPABASE) ================= --> <script> // ------------------------------------------------------------- // 1. sessionInitializationPromise = initialization; return sessionInitializationPromise; } // ------------------------------------------------------------- // INACTIVIDAD (solo asesoras): aviso a 4 min, cierre a 5, multi-pestaña // ------------------------------------------------------------- const IDLE_WARN_MS = 240000, IDLE_LIMIT_MS = 300000, IDLE_KEY = 'idleLastActivityAt'; let agentSessionId = null, idleWarnTimer = null, idleTickTimer = null, idleListenersOn = false, idleLastTouchSent = 0; function idleLastTs() { return Number(localStorage.getItem(IDLE_KEY) || 0); } function isIdleMonitored() { return !!agentSessionId && currentAccess?.role === 'agente'; } function hideIdleBanner() { document.getElementById('idleBanner')?.classList.add('hidden'); } function showIdleBanner() { if (!isIdleMonitored()) return; document.getElementById('idleBanner')?.classList.remove('hidden'); if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons(); } function idleSecondsLeft() { return Math.max(0, Math.ceil((IDLE_LIMIT_MS - (Date.now() - idleLastTs())) / 1000)); } function continueAgentSession() { markUserActivity(); } function markUserActivity() { if (!isIdleMonitored()) return; localStorage.setItem(IDLE_KEY, String(Date.now())); hideIdleBanner(); restartIdleTimers(); touchAgentSessionThrottled(); } function idleStorageSync(event) { if (event.key === IDLE_KEY && event.newValue) { hideIdleBanner(); restartIdleTimers(); } } async function touchAgentSessionThrottled() { if (!isIdleMonitored() || !supabaseClient) return; if (Date.now() - idleLastTouchSent < 60000) return; idleLastTouchSent = Date.now(); const generation = sessionGeneration, userId = currentUser?.id, accessUpdatedAt = currentAccess?.updated_at, sid = agentSessionId; try { await supabaseClient.rpc('touch_agent_session', { p_session_id: sid }); } catch (_) { /* la UI no se bloquea por telemetría */ } if (generation !== sessionGeneration || currentUser?.id !== userId || currentAccess?.updated_at !== accessUpdatedAt) return; } function restartIdleTimers() { clearTimeout(idleWarnTimer); const el = document.getElementById('idleCountdown'); if (el) el.textContent = String(left); if (left <= 0) { endAgentSessionForIdle(); return; } idleTickTimer = setTimeout(tick, 1000); }; tick(); }, Math.max(0, IDLE_WARN_MS - (Date.now() - idleLastTs()))); } async function endAgentSessionForIdle() { if (!isIdleMonitored()) return; const generation = sessionGeneration, sid = agentSessionId; stopIdleTracking(); if (supabaseClient) { try { await supabaseClient.rpc('end_agent_session', { p_session_id: sid, p_reason: 'idle_timeout' }); } catch (_) { /* cierre best-effort: no bloquea el flujo */ } } if (generation !== sessionGeneration) return; agentSessionId = null; await closeInvalidSession('Tu sesión se cerró por 5 minutos de inactividad.', 'idle_timeout'); } // Solo esta pestaña termina; otras del mismo dispositivo conservan su copia. async function endAgentSessionLocally(reason) { const sid = agentSessionId; if (!sid || !supabaseClient) return; try { await supabaseClient.rpc('end_agent_session', { p_session_id: sid, p_reason: reason }); } catch (_) { /* cierre best-effort: no bloquea el flujo */ } if (agentSessionId === sid) agentSessionId = null; } function stopIdleTracking() { clearTimeout(idleWarnTimer); clearTimeout(idleTickTimer); idleWarnTimer = null; idleTickTimer = null; if (idleListenersOn && typeof window !== 'undefined') { ['pointerdown', 'keydown', 'scroll', 'visibilitychange'].forEach(e => window.removeEventListener(e, markUserActivity, true)); window.removeEventListener('storage', idleStorageSync); } idleListenersOn = false; } async function startAgentActivityTracking() { if (!supabaseClient || currentAccess?.role !== 'agente') return; const generation = sessionGeneration, userId = currentUser?.id, accessUpdatedAt = currentAccess?.updated_at; window.addEventListener('storage', idleStorageSync); idleListenersOn = true; } restartIdleTimers(); } catch (_) { /* sesión operativa no registrada; la app sigue funcionando */ } } function idleMiami(ts) { if (!ts) return '-'; const d = new Date(ts); return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? '-' : d.toLocaleString('es-ES', { timeZone: 'America/New_York', hour12: false, year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }); } function isRecentAgentSession(session, now = Date.now()) { const lastActivity = Date.parse(session.last_activity_at || session.started_at || ''); return Boolean(session.active) && Number.isFinite(lastActivity) && now - lastActivity < IDLE_LIMIT_MS; } async function loadAgentSessionsReport() { const box = document.getElementById('agentSessionsBody'); if (!box || !isAdmin || !supabaseClient) return; const generation = sessionGeneration, userId = currentUser?.id, accessUpdatedAt = currentAccess?.updated_at; box.innerHTML = '<p role="status">Cargando actividad…</p>'; const { data, error } = await supabaseClient.rpc('admin_agent_sessions_report', {}); if (!box || generation !== sessionGeneration || currentUser?.id !== userId || currentAccess?.updated_at !== accessUpdatedAt) return; if (error) { box.innerHTML = '<div role="alert" class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-rose-300"><p>No se pudo cargar la actividad.</p><button type="button" onclick="loadAgentSessionsReport()" class="min-h-11 rounded-lg border border-rose-400 px-3 py-2 font-semibold hover:bg-rose-400/10 focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-rose-300">Reintentar</button></div>'; return; } const sessions = Array.isArray(data) ? Si en otra versión no está, o su cuerpo ya fue leído, se pasa // de largo y se usa un mensaje genérico: esto nunca debe lanzar. async function readFunctionErrorCode(error) { const context = error?.context; if (!context || typeof context.json !== 'function') return null; try { const body = await context.json(); return typeof body?.error === 'string' ? body.error : null; } catch (err) { console.warn('No se pudo leer la respuesta de la función:', err); return null; } } async function handleChangeEmail(event) { event.preventDefault(); if (!isAdmin || !changeEmailTarget) return; const newEmail = (document.getElementById('changeEmailNew')?.value || '').trim().toLowerCase(); const status = document.getElementById('changeEmailStatus'); const btn = event.target.querySelector('button[type="submit"]'); if (btn) btn.disabled = true; if (status) status.textContent = 'Enviando confirmaciones...'; try { const { data, error } = await supabaseClient.functions.invoke('change-user-email', { body: { targetUserId: changeEmailTarget.userId, newEmail }, }); if (!error && data?.accepted) { closeChangeEmail(); showToast('Confirmaciones enviadas al correo nuevo y al antiguo', 'success'); return; } const code = (typeof data?.error === 'string' ? data.error : null) ?? await readFunctionErrorCode(error); if (status) status.textContent = changeEmailMessages[code] || 'No se pudo solicitar el cambio de correo.'; } catch (err) { console.warn('No se pudo solicitar el cambio de correo:', err); if (status) status.textContent = 'No se pudo solicitar el cambio de correo.'; } finally { if (btn) btn.disabled = false; } } async function setUserActive(userId, activo) { if (!isAdmin) return; const { error } = await supabaseClient.rpc('admin_manage_user_access', { p_action: 'set_active', p_user_id: userId, p_activo: activo }); if (error) showToast('No se pudo actualizar el usuario', 'error'); else { showToast(activo ? La promesa se memoriza en la // propia funcion, sin variables de modulo. function ensureChartJs() { if (window.Chart) return Promise.resolve(true); if (!ensureChartJs.promise) { ensureChartJs.promise = new Promise((resolver) => { const script = document.createElement('script'); script.src = 'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js@4.5.1'; script.onload = () => resolver(true); script.onerror = () => { ensureChartJs.promise = null; resolver(false); }; document.head.appendChild(script); }); } return ensureChartJs.promise; } function updateChartTheme() { if (!window.Chart) return; const light = document.documentElement.dataset.theme === 'light'; const text = light ? } } catch (e) { console.warn('Error navegando el calendario:', e); } } function renderCitas() { const result=document.getElementById('citasResultado')?.value||'', camp=document.getElementById('citasCampana')?.value||'', medio=document.getElementById('citasMedio')?.value||'', advisor=isAdmin || isSupervisor?(document.getElementById('citasAsesor')?.value||''):''; CARGA DE DATOS ESTRICTAMENTE DESDE SUPABASE // ------------------------------------------------------------- // compareLeadIdsDescending vive en src/legacy/leads.js. function consolidateLeadsById(leads) { const leadsById = new Map(); leads.forEach(lead => { if (!lead || lead.id === undefined || lead.id === null) { throw new Error("Supabase devolvió un lead sin id"); } const key = String(lead.id); leadsById.set(key, { ...(leadsById.get(key) || {}), ...lead }); }); return Array.from(leadsById.values()).sort(compareLeadIdsDescending); } function upsertLeadInState(lead) { if (!lead || lead.id === undefined || lead.id === null) return false; const leadId = String(lead.id); const existingLead = allLeads.find(item => String(item.id) === leadId); const cachedLead = completeCurrentLoaded ? completeCurrentLeads.find(item => String(item.id) === leadId) : undefined; if (!existingLead && cachedLead) { completeCurrentLeads = consolidateLeadsById([ ...completeCurrentLeads.filter(item => String(item.id) !== leadId), { ...cachedLead, ...lead } ]); leadDataRevision += 1; markPeriodoReportDirty(); return false; } allLeads = consolidateLeadsById([ ...allLeads.filter(item => String(item.id) !== leadId), { ...(existingLead || {}), ...lead } ]); if (allLeads.length > loadedLeadTarget) allLeads.length = loadedLeadTarget; if (completeCurrentLoaded) { completeCurrentLeads = consolidateLeadsById([ ...completeCurrentLeads.filter(item => String(item.id) !== leadId), { ...(cachedLead || {}), ...lead } ]); } if (!existingLead) totalDatabaseCount += 1; leadDataRevision += 1; markPeriodoReportDirty(); return !existingLead; } function removeLeadFromState(leadId) { if (leadId === undefined || leadId === null) return false; const previousLength = allLeads.length; allLeads = allLeads.filter(lead => String(lead.id) !== String(leadId)); const removedVisible = allLeads.length !== previousLength; let removedCached = false; if (completeCurrentLoaded) { const previousCacheLength = completeCurrentLeads.length; completeCurrentLeads = completeCurrentLeads.filter(lead => String(lead.id) !== String(leadId)); removedCached = completeCurrentLeads.length !== previousCacheLength; } if (!removedVisible && !removedCached) return false; totalDatabaseCount = Math.max(0, totalDatabaseCount - 1); leadDataRevision += 1; markPeriodoReportDirty(); return removedVisible; } // withOrigin y getLeadOrigin viven en src/legacy/leads.js (etiqueta __origin // no enumerable: los ids numericos colisionan entre tablas). function findLeadByOrigin(origin, leadId) { if (leadId === undefined || leadId === null) return undefined; const arr = origin === 'historico' ? allHistorico : completeCurrentLoaded ? completeCurrentLeads : allLeads; return arr.find(l => String(l.id) === String(leadId)); } function originYear(origin) { return origin === 'historico' ? '2025' : '2026'; } // Base visible según el selector de origen (allLeads sigue siendo solo actuales). function getVisibleBaseLeads() { if (dataSource === 'historico') return allHistorico; if (dataSource === 'todos') return [...(completeCurrentLoaded ? completeCurrentLeads : allLeads), ...allHistorico]; return allLeads; } function getHistoricalBaseLeads() { const current = completeCurrentLoaded ? completeCurrentLeads : allLeads; if (dataSource === 'historico') return allHistorico; if (dataSource === 'todos') return [...current, ...allHistorico]; return current; } async function setDataSource(value) { const previousSource = dataSource; dataSource = value === 'historico' ? 'historico' : value === 'todos' ? 'todos' : 'actual'; const select = document.getElementById('dataSourceFilter'); if (select && select.value !== dataSource) select.value = dataSource; if (dataSource !== 'actual') { showToast('Cargando datos del origen seleccionado…', 'info'); const tasks = [loadHistoricoData()]; if (dataSource === 'todos') tasks.push(loadCompleteCurrentData()); const results = await Promise.all(tasks); if (results.some(result => result !== true)) { dataSource = previousSource; if (select) select.value = previousSource; showToast('No se pudo cargar el origen completo.', 'error'); return; } } else if (currentMainTab === 'historico') { if (!await loadCompleteCurrentData()) { dataSource = previousSource; if (select) select.value = previousSource; return; } } populateFilterOptions(); applyFilters(); updateLeadScopeControls(); if (currentMainTab === 'historico') renderHistoricalAnalytics(); } async function getExactLeadsCount() { const { count, error } = await supabaseClient .from('leads') .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true }) .is('archived_at', null); if (error) throw new Error("Error contando registros en Supabase: " + error.message); if (!Number.isInteger(count) || count < 0) { throw new Error("Supabase no devolvió un conteo exacto válido"); } return count; } // Cursor canónico por id; cada página reconstruye la consulta filtrada. async function fetchRowsByIdCursor(makeQuery, options = {}) { const blockSize = 1000; const rows = []; const maxRows = Number.isFinite(options.maxRows) ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(options.maxRows)) : Infinity; let cursor = options.startAfterId ?? null; const cursors = new Set(); const generation = sessionGeneration, userId = currentUser?.id, accessUpdatedAt = currentAccess?.updated_at; const sessionValid = () => generation === sessionGeneration && currentUser?.id === userId && currentAccess?.updated_at === accessUpdatedAt; while (true) { if (rows.length >= maxRows) break; if (!sessionValid()) throw new Error('La sesión cambió durante la paginación'); let query = makeQuery(); if (cursor !== null) query = query.lt('id', cursor); const pageSize = Math.min(blockSize, maxRows - rows.length); query = query .order('id', { ascending: false }) .range(0, pageSize - 1); const { data, error } = await query; if (!sessionValid()) throw new Error('La sesión cambió durante la paginación'); if (error) { const failure = new Error(`Error cargando bloque posterior a ${cursor ?? 'inicio'}: ${error.message}`); failure.code = error.code; throw failure; } if (!Array.isArray(data) || data.length > blockSize) { throw new Error("Supabase devolvió un bloque inválido"); } if (data.length === 0) break; const nextCursor = data[data.length - 1]?.id; if (nextCursor === undefined || nextCursor === null || cursors.has(String(nextCursor))) { throw new Error("La paginación por id no pudo avanzar"); } rows.push(...data); cursor = nextCursor; cursors.add(String(cursor)); // max_rows del servidor puede ser menor que blockSize: solo una // página vacía confirma que no quedan filas tras el cursor. } return rows; } // Conserva el contrato {data,error} usado por paneles y sus guardas de sesión. async function fetchPagedResult(makeQuery) { try { return { data: await fetchRowsByIdCursor(makeQuery), error: null }; } catch (error) { return { data: null, error }; } } // Lotes pequeños evitan URLs excesivas en .in(); el cursor evita truncamiento. async function fetchRowsByIds(table, columns, ids, options = {}) { const rows = []; for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i += 200) { const batch = ids.slice(i, i + 200); const result = await fetchPagedResult(() => { let query = supabaseClient.from(table).select(columns).in('id', batch); if (options.excludeArchived) query = query.is('archived_at', null); return query; }); if (result.error) return result; rows.push(...result.data); } return { data: rows, error: null }; } async function fetchLeadsByIdCursor(columns = '*', options = {}) { return consolidateLeadsById(await fetchRowsByIdCursor(() => supabaseClient.from('leads').select(columns).is('archived_at', null), options)); return removed || cached; } return false; } // ------------------------------------------------------------- // 4. FILTROS Y BÚSQUEDA // ------------------------------------------------------------- function populateFilterOptions() { const getUniqueValues = (key) => { const set = new Set(); getVisibleBaseLeads().forEach(item => { const val = getField(item, key).trim(); if (val && val !== 'SN') set.add(val); }); return Array.from(set).sort(); }; populateSelect('filterMes', catalogOptions('mes', getUniqueValues('Mes'))); populateSelect('filterCampana', catalogOptions('campana', getUniqueValues('Campaña'))); populateSelect('filterMedio', catalogOptions('medio', getUniqueValues('Medio'))); populateSelect('filterGestion', catalogOptions('gestion', getUniqueValues('GESTION'))); populateSelect('filterAsesora', catalogOptions('agente', getUniqueValues('Agente '))); } function populateSelect(elementId, values) { const select = document.getElementById(elementId); if (!select) return; const currentVal = select.value; const firstOption = select.options[0]; select.innerHTML = ''; select.appendChild(firstOption); values.forEach(v => { const opt = document.createElement('option'); opt.value = v; opt.innerText = catalogLabel(v); if (v === currentVal) opt.selected = true; select.appendChild(opt); }); } // ------------------------------------------------------------- // 4b. CATÁLOGOS (lead_catalogs): valores admin ∪ valores en datos // ------------------------------------------------------------- async function loadCatalogs() { const generation = sessionGeneration, userId = currentUser?.id, accessUpdatedAt = currentAccess?.updated_at; const valid = () => generation === sessionGeneration && currentUser?.id === userId && currentAccess?.updated_at === accessUpdatedAt && currentAccess?.activo; if (!supabaseClient || !valid()) return false; catalogRows = { mes: [], campana: [], medio: [], gestion: [], agente: [] }; try { const { data, error } = await supabaseClient .from('lead_catalogs') .select('id, kind, value, active') .order('id'); if (!valid()) return false; // Migración 0009 no aplicada o error: degrada a valores deducidos de los datos. if (error || !data) return false; data.forEach(r => { if (catalogRows[r.kind]) catalogRows[r.kind].push(r); }); return true; } catch (error) { if (!valid()) return false; throw error; } } function activeCatalogValues(kind) { return (catalogRows[kind] || []).filter(r => r.active).map(r => r.value); } // Opciones de un filtro: valores activos del catálogo + valores presentes // en los datos que el catálogo no conoce. Un valor desactivado en el // catálogo queda oculto aunque aparezca en los leads. Orden de salida: // alfabético en español; el kind 'mes' mantiene el orden cronológico. function catalogOptions(kind, dataValues) { const known = new Set((catalogRows[kind] || []).map(r => r.value)); // Los valores de los datos pueden venir repetidos (p. ej. la misma campaña // en varias citas) y no hay que convertirlos en varias opciones del menú. const extra = (dataValues || []).filter(v => !known.has(v)); const all = [...new Set([...activeCatalogValues(kind), ...extra])]; return kind === 'mes' ? sortMonthCatalog(all) : sortAlphaEs(all); } // Rellena un <select> desde el catálogo preservando la selección actual // (y el valor actual aunque no esté en el catálogo). Vacío → no toca el HTML. function fillSelectFromCatalog(id, values) { const select = document.getElementById(id); if (!select || !values.length) return; // Compara formatos ('SEPTIEMBRE' vs 'Septiembre') y conserva la forma // canónica del catálogo; sin equivalente, conserva el valor actual. const key = v => String(v == null ? '' : v).trim().toLocaleLowerCase('es'); const current = select.value; const canonical = current ? values.find(v => key(v) === key(current)) : undefined; const selected = canonical !== undefined ? canonical : current; select.innerHTML = ''; const all = current && canonical === undefined ? [...values, current] : values; all.forEach(v => { const opt = document.createElement('option'); opt.value = v; opt.innerText = catalogLabel(v); if (v === selected) opt.selected = true; select.appendChild(opt); }); } function fillDatalistFromCatalog(id, values) { const dl = document.getElementById(id); if (!dl || !values.length) return; dl.innerHTML = values.map(v => `<option value="${escapeAttr(v)}">`).join(''); } function populateCatalogSelects() { // Mes conserva orden cronológico; el resto de catálogos sale alfabético. const mes = sortMonthCatalog(activeCatalogValues('mes')); const campana = sortAlphaEs(activeCatalogValues('campana')); const medio = sortAlphaEs(activeCatalogValues('medio')); const gestion = sortAlphaEs(activeCatalogValues('gestion')); // Formulario Nuevo fillSelectFromCatalog('newMes', mes); fillSelectFromCatalog('newCampana', campana); fillSelectFromCatalog('newMedio', medio); fillSelectFromCatalog('newGestion', gestion); // Formulario Editar fillSelectFromCatalog('editGestion', gestion); fillDatalistFromCatalog('mesList', mes); fillDatalistFromCatalog('campanaEditList', campana); fillDatalistFromCatalog('medioList', medio); // Asesores (compartido por Nuevo y Editar) fillDatalistFromCatalog('agenteList', sortAlphaEs(activeCatalogValues('agente'))); } async function refreshCatalogs() { const generation = sessionGeneration, userId = currentUser?.id, accessUpdatedAt = currentAccess?.updated_at; await loadCatalogs(); if (generation !== sessionGeneration || currentUser?.id !== userId || currentAccess?.updated_at !== accessUpdatedAt || !currentAccess?.activo) return; populateCatalogSelects(); populateFilterOptions(); applyFilters(); markPeriodoReportDirty(); } function openCatalogsModal() { if (!isAdmin) return; const modal = document.getElementById('modalCatalogs'); if (modal) { modal.classList.remove('hidden'); modal.classList.add('flex'); renderCatalogList(); if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons(); } } function closeCatalogsModal() { const modal = document.getElementById('modalCatalogs'); if (modal) { modal.classList.add('hidden'); modal.classList.remove('flex'); } } function renderCatalogList() { const kind = document.getElementById('catalogKind')?.value || 'mes'; const container = document.getElementById('catalogList'); if (!container) return; const rows = catalogRows[kind] || []; if (!rows.length) { container.innerHTML = '<p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 py-2">Sin valores. Si la migración 0009 no está aplicada, los filtros usan los valores de los datos.</p>'; return; } container.innerHTML = rows.map(r => ` <div class="catalog-row"> <span class="catalog-row-value ${r.active ? '' : 'is-inactive'}">${escapeHtml(r.value)}</span> <div class="catalog-row-actions"> <button type="button" onclick="renameCatalogValue(${Number(r.id)})" title="Renombrar" aria-label="Renombrar ${escapeAttr(r.value)}"><i data-lucide="pencil" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button> <button type="button" onclick="toggleCatalogValue(${Number(r.id)}, ${r.active ? 'false' : 'true'})" title="${r.active ? 'Desactivar' : 'Reactivar'}" aria-label="${r.active ? 'Desactivar' : 'Reactivar'} ${escapeAttr(r.value)}"><i data-lucide="${r.active ? 'x-circle' : 'check-circle'}" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button> </div> </div>`).join(''); if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons(); } async function addCatalogValue() { if (!isAdmin) return; const kind = document.getElementById('catalogKind')?.value || 'mes'; const input = document.getElementById('catalogNewValue'); const value = (input?.value || '').trim(); if (!value) { showToast('Escribe un valor', 'warning'); return; } const { error } = await supabaseClient.rpc('create_lead_catalog', { p_kind: kind, p_value: value }); if (error) { showToast('No se pudo añadir el valor', 'error'); return; } if (input) input.value = ''; await refreshCatalogs(); renderCatalogList(); showToast('Valor añadido', 'success'); } async function renameCatalogValue(id) { if (!isAdmin) return; const rows = Object.values(catalogRows).flat(); const row = rows.find(r => Number(r.id) === Number(id)); const next = prompt('Renombrar valor:', row ? row.value : ''); if (next === null) return; const trimmed = next.trim(); if (!trimmed) { showToast('El valor no puede estar vacío', 'warning'); return; } const { error } = await supabaseClient.rpc('rename_lead_catalog', { p_id: id, p_value: trimmed }); if (error) { showToast('No se pudo renombrar (¿valor duplicado en la lista?)', 'error'); return; } await refreshCatalogs(); renderCatalogList(); showToast('Valor renombrado', 'success'); } async function toggleCatalogValue(id, active) { if (!isAdmin) return; const { error } = await supabaseClient.rpc('set_lead_catalog_active', { p_id: id, p_active: active }); if (error) { showToast('No se pudo actualizar el valor', 'error'); return; } await refreshCatalogs(); renderCatalogList(); showToast(active ? 'Valor reactivado' : 'Valor desactivado', 'success'); } let filterDebounceTimer = null; function scheduleApplyFilters() { clearTimeout(filterDebounceTimer); filterDebounceTimer = setTimeout(applyFilters, 200); } let dashRangeGen = 0; function clearDashRange() { const desde = document.getElementById('dashDesde'); const hasta = document.getElementById('dashHasta'); const err = document.getElementById('dashRangeError'); if (desde) desde.value = ''; if (hasta) hasta.value = ''; if (err) { err.classList.add('hidden'); err.innerText = ''; } applyFilters(); } // Rango de gestión activo: base global temporal. Consulta lead_gestiones // de todo el período y descarga solo los leads con gestión en esas // fechas (aunque estén fuera de la ventana móvil de 2000). No toca // allLeads ni el histórico; al limpiar el rango vuelve la ventana. // Devuelve null si la sesión caducó; lanza si la consulta falla. async function loadLeadsByGestionRange(desde, hasta) { const generation = sessionGeneration, userId = currentUser?.id, accessUpdatedAt = currentAccess?.updated_at; const valid = () => generation === sessionGeneration && currentUser?.id === userId && currentAccess?.updated_at === accessUpdatedAt; if (!valid()) return null; const key = `${desde}|${hasta}|${leadDataRevision}`; if (key === gestionRangeKey) return gestionRangeLeads; // Comparar fechas planas YYYY-MM-DD; el navegador no decide la fecha const { data, error } = await fetchPagedResult(() => supabaseClient .from('lead_gestiones') .select('id,lead_id') .gte('fecha_gestion', desde) .lte('fecha_gestion', hasta)); if (!valid()) return null; if (error) throw error; const ids = [...new Set((data || []).map(r => r.lead_id).filter(Boolean))]; let leads = []; if (ids.length) { const res = await fetchRowsByIds('leads', '*', ids, { excludeArchived: true }); if (!valid()) return null; if (res.error) throw res.error; leads = consolidateLeadsById(res.data || []); } gestionRangeKey = key; gestionRangeLeads = leads; return leads; } // Mes seleccionado con origen actual/todos: base global temporal por mes. // Consulta todos los leads actuales con ese Mes aunque estén fuera de la // ventana móvil de 2000. No toca allLeads ni el histórico; al quitar el // mes vuelve la ventana. Devuelve null si la sesión caducó. async function loadLeadsByMonth(mes) { const generation = sessionGeneration, userId = currentUser?.id, accessUpdatedAt = currentAccess?.updated_at; const valid = () => generation === sessionGeneration && currentUser?.id === userId && currentAccess?.updated_at === accessUpdatedAt; if (!valid()) return null; const key = `${mes}|${leadDataRevision}`; if (key === mesRangeKey) return mesRangeLeads; // Los valores de Mes en la base están limpios (sin espacios): eq exacto. const { data, error } = await fetchPagedResult(() => supabaseClient .from('leads') .select('*') .eq('Mes', mes) .is('archived_at', null)); if (!valid()) return null; if (error) throw error; const leads = consolidateLeadsById(data || []); mesRangeKey = key; mesRangeLeads = leads; return leads; } async function applyFilters() { clearTimeout(filterDebounceTimer); // Invalidar cualquier consulta del rango aún en vuelo const gen = ++dashRangeGen; const generation = sessionGeneration, userId = currentUser?.id, accessUpdatedAt = currentAccess?.updated_at; const valid = () => gen === dashRangeGen && generation === sessionGeneration && currentUser?.id === userId && currentAccess?.updated_at === accessUpdatedAt; const search = (document.getElementById('filterSearch')?.value || '').toLowerCase().trim(); const mes = document.getElementById('filterMes')?.value || ''; const campana = document.getElementById('filterCampana')?.value || ''; const medio = document.getElementById('filterMedio')?.value || ''; const gestion = document.getElementById('filterGestion')?.value || ''; const asesora = document.getElementById('filterAsesora')?.value || ''; const dashDesde = document.getElementById('dashDesde')?.value || ''; const dashHasta = document.getElementById('dashHasta')?.value || ''; const errEl = document.getElementById('dashRangeError'); const scopeEl = document.getElementById('dashRangeScope'); // Validar rango de fechas de gestión let gestionBase = null; // conjunto global temporal: leads con gestión en el período if (dashDesde || dashHasta) { if (!dashDesde || !dashHasta || dashDesde > dashHasta) { if (errEl) { errEl.classList.remove('hidden'); errEl.innerText = 'Completa Desde y Hasta en orden válido; el rango no se ha aplicado.'; } if (scopeEl) { scopeEl.classList.add('hidden'); scopeEl.innerText = ''; } return; } if (errEl) { errEl.classList.add('hidden'); errEl.innerText = ''; } if (!supabaseClient || !currentUser) { if (errEl) { errEl.classList.remove('hidden'); errEl.innerText = 'No se pudo consultar la base de datos. Verifica la sesión.'; } return; } try { gestionBase = await loadLeadsByGestionRange(dashDesde, dashHasta); if (!valid() || gestionBase === null) return; // sesión obsoleta: no pintar parcial if (dataSource !== 'actual' && errEl) { errEl.classList.remove('hidden'); errEl.innerText = 'El rango de gestión solo incluye leads actuales; el histórico no tiene historial de gestiones.'; } if (scopeEl) { scopeEl.classList.remove('hidden'); scopeEl.innerText = `Global por gestión · ${gestionBase.length.toLocaleString()} leads`; } } catch (e) { if (!valid()) return; console.warn('Error consultando lead_gestiones por rango:', e); if (errEl) { errEl.classList.remove('hidden'); errEl.innerText = 'Error al consultar el rango de gestión. Inténtalo de nuevo.'; } if (scopeEl) { scopeEl.classList.add('hidden'); scopeEl.innerText = ''; } return; } } else { if (errEl) { errEl.classList.add('hidden'); errEl.innerText = ''; } if (scopeEl) { scopeEl.classList.add('hidden'); scopeEl.innerText = ''; } } // Mes seleccionado con origen actual/todos: base global temporal por mes. // Con rango de gestión activo el mes se aplica localmente sobre ese // conjunto (una sola descarga); en histórico la colección ya es completa. let mesBase = null; if (mes !== '' && gestionBase === null && dataSource !== 'historico') { if (!supabaseClient || !currentUser) { showToast('No se pudo consultar la base de datos. Verifica la sesión.', 'error'); return; // nada parcial presentado como global } try { mesBase = await loadLeadsByMonth(mes); if (!valid() || mesBase === null) return; // sesión obsoleta: no pintar parcial } catch (e) { if (!valid()) return; console.warn('Error consultando leads por mes:', e); showToast('Error al consultar el mes. Inténtalo de nuevo.', 'error'); return; } } // Con rango activo la base es el conjunto global por gestión (ya // excluye históricos y archivados); con mes, el global del mes (más el // histórico en el origen «Todos»); sin ninguno, la ventana móvil o el // origen seleccionado. const baseLeads = gestionBase !== null ? gestionBase : mesBase !== null ? (dataSource === 'todos' ? [...mesBase, ...allHistorico] : mesBase) : getVisibleBaseLeads(); filteredLeads = baseLeads.filter(lead => { if (!currentUser || !currentAccess?.activo) return; if (lead.archived_at) return false; if (search) { const nombre = getField(lead, 'Nombre').toLowerCase(); const telefono = getField(lead, 'Telefono').toLowerCase(); const observaciones = getField(lead, 'OBSERVACIONES ').toLowerCase(); if (!nombre.includes(search) && !telefono.includes(search) && !observaciones.includes(search)) { return false; } } if (mes && getField(lead, 'Mes').trim() !== mes) return false; if (campana && getField(lead, 'Campaña').trim() !== campana) return false; if (medio && getField(lead, 'Medio').trim() !== medio) return false; if (gestion && getField(lead, 'GESTION').trim() !== gestion) return false; const agenteLead = getField(lead, 'Agente ').trim() || getField(lead, 'Agente').trim(); if (asesora && agenteLead !== asesora) return false; return true; }); // Contar el rango Desde-Hasta como un solo filtro const rangeActive = dashDesde !== '' || dashHasta !== ''; const activeCount = [mes, campana, medio, gestion, asesora, search, rangeActive].filter(Boolean).length; const activeFiltersCountEl = document.getElementById('activeFiltersCount'); if (activeFiltersCountEl) { activeFiltersCountEl.innerText = activeCount > 0 ? `${activeCount} filtro${activeCount > 1 ? 's' : ''} activo${activeCount > 1 ? 's' : ''}` : 'Sin filtros'; } currentPage = 1; updateKpis(); renderMatrix(); renderTable(); } function resetAllFilters() { if (document.getElementById('filterSearch')) document.getElementById('filterSearch').value = ''; if (document.getElementById('filterMes')) document.getElementById('filterMes').value = ''; if (document.getElementById('filterCampana')) document.getElementById('filterCampana').value = ''; if (document.getElementById('filterMedio')) document.getElementById('filterMedio').value = ''; if (document.getElementById('filterGestion')) document.getElementById('filterGestion').value = ''; if (document.getElementById('filterAsesora')) document.getElementById('filterAsesora').value = ''; if (document.getElementById('dashDesde')) document.getElementById('dashDesde').value = ''; if (document.getElementById('dashHasta')) document.getElementById('dashHasta').value = ''; const err = document.getElementById('dashRangeError'); if (err) { err.classList.add('hidden'); err.innerText = ''; } applyFilters(); showToast("Filtros reiniciados", "info"); } // ------------------------------------------------------------- // 5. CÁLCULO DE KPIS // ------------------------------------------------------------- function updateKpis() { const total = filteredLeads.length; const totalDatabase = totalDatabaseCount; const tabTotalEl = document.getElementById('tabTotalDatabaseCount'); if (tabTotalEl) { tabTotalEl.innerText = totalDatabase.toLocaleString(); } const agendados = filteredLeads.filter(l => { const g = getField(l, 'GESTION').toLowerCase(); return g.includes('agendad'); }).length; const inscritos = filteredLeads.filter(l => { const g = getField(l, 'GESTION').toLowerCase(); return g.includes('inscrit'); }).length; const conversion = total > 0 ? ((inscritos / total) * 100).toFixed(1) : "0.0"; const agendadosPct = total > 0 ? ((agendados / total) * 100).toFixed(1) : "0.0"; document.getElementById('kpiTotalLeads').innerText = total.toLocaleString(); const kpiTotalSubtitle = document.getElementById('kpiTotalSubtitle'); if (kpiTotalSubtitle) { const hasFilters = document.getElementById('activeFiltersCount')?.innerText !== 'Sin filtros'; const dashDesde = document.getElementById('dashDesde')?.value || ''; const dashHasta = document.getElementById('dashHasta')?.value || ''; const mesSel = document.getElementById('filterMes')?.value || ''; if (dashDesde !== '' || dashHasta !== '') { kpiTotalSubtitle.innerText = 'Resultados globales por gestión'; } else if (mesSel !== '' && dataSource !== 'historico') { kpiTotalSubtitle.innerText = 'Resultados globales del mes'; } else if (hasFilters) { kpiTotalSubtitle.innerText = `Resultados dentro de ${getVisibleBaseLeads().length.toLocaleString()} cargados`; } else { kpiTotalSubtitle.innerText = loadedLeadTarget <= LEADS_BATCH_SIZE ? 'Últimos registros ingresados' : 'Incluye bloques anteriores'; } } document.getElementById('kpiAgendados').innerText = agendados.toLocaleString(); document.getElementById('kpiAgendadosPct').innerText = `${agendadosPct}%`; document.getElementById('kpiInscritos').innerText = inscritos.toLocaleString(); document.getElementById('kpiConversionRate').innerText = `${conversion}%`; document.getElementById('leadsTableCount').innerText = `(${total.toLocaleString()})`; } // ------------------------------------------------------------- // 6. MATRIZ CRUZADA ANALÍTICA // ------------------------------------------------------------- function renderMatrix() { const thead = document.getElementById('matrixThead'); const tbody = document.getElementById('matrixTbody'); const tfoot = document.getElementById('matrixTfoot'); if (!thead || !tbody || !tfoot) return; const gestionesMap = new Map(); const campanasMap = new Map(); filteredLeads.forEach(l => { const g = getField(l, 'GESTION').trim() || 'Sin Definir'; const c = getField(l, 'Campaña').trim() || 'General'; gestionesMap.set(g, (gestionesMap.get(g) || 0) + 1); campanasMap.set(c, (campanasMap.get(c) || 0) + 1); }); // Matriz ordenada alfabéticamente (filas y columnas); solo presentación. const sortedGestiones = Array.from(gestionesMap.keys()).sort(compareAlphaEs); const sortedCampanas = Array.from(campanasMap.keys()) .filter(c => c !== '') .sort(compareAlphaEs); let headerHtml = `<tr> <th class="px-4 py-3 bg-slate-950 font-bold text-slate-200 border-r border-slate-800">Estatus (GESTIÓN)</th>`; sortedCampanas.forEach(camp => { headerHtml += `<th class="px-3 py-3 text-center whitespace-nowrap text-slate-300 font-semibold">${escapeHtml(camp)}</th>`; }); headerHtml += `<th class="px-4 py-3 text-right bg-slate-950 font-bold text-slate-100 border-l border-slate-800">Total Fila</th></tr>`; thead.innerHTML = headerHtml; const matrixCounts = {}; const colTotals = {}; sortedCampanas.forEach(c => colTotals[c] = 0); filteredLeads.forEach(l => { const g = getField(l, 'GESTION').trim() || 'Sin Definir'; const c = getField(l, 'Campaña').trim() || 'General'; const cellKey = `${g}:::${c}`; matrixCounts[cellKey] = (matrixCounts[cellKey] || 0) + 1; if (colTotals[c] !== undefined) colTotals[c]++; }); let bodyHtml = ''; sortedGestiones.forEach(gestionName => { let rowTotal = 0; const isClosedInscrito = gestionName.toLowerCase().includes('inscrit'); const isAgendado = gestionName.toLowerCase().includes('agendad'); bodyHtml += `<tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/60 transition-colors"> <td class="px-4 py-2.5 font-medium border-r border-slate-800 flex items-center gap-2"> <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${isClosedInscrito ? 'bg-emerald-400' : isAgendado ? 'bg-amber-400' : 'bg-slate-500'}"></span> <span class="truncate max-w-[180px]">${escapeHtml(gestionName)}</span> </td>`; sortedCampanas.forEach(campName => { const count = matrixCounts[`${gestionName}:::${campName}`] || 0; rowTotal += count; let cellClass = "px-3 py-2 text-center tabular-nums cursor-pointer transition-colors "; if (count === 0) { cellClass += "text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-800/40"; } else if (isClosedInscrito) { cellClass += "text-emerald-400 font-semibold bg-emerald-500/10 hover:bg-emerald-500/20"; } else if (isAgendado) { cellClass += "text-amber-300 font-semibold bg-amber-500/10 hover:bg-amber-500/20"; } else { cellClass += "text-slate-200 font-medium hover:bg-indigo-500/10"; } bodyHtml += `<td class="${cellClass}" data-matrix-gestion="${escapeAttr(gestionName)}" data-matrix-campana="${escapeAttr(campName)}"> ${count > 0 ? count.toLocaleString() : '-'} </td>`; }); bodyHtml += `<td class="px-4 py-2.5 text-right font-mono font-bold border-l border-slate-800 text-slate-100 tabular-nums"> ${rowTotal.toLocaleString()} </td></tr>`; }); tbody.innerHTML = bodyHtml; let grandTotal = 0; let footHtml = `<tr> <td class="px-4 py-3 font-bold text-slate-100 border-r border-slate-800 uppercase tracking-wider">Total Campaña</td>`; sortedCampanas.forEach(campName => { const colSum = colTotals[campName] || 0; grandTotal += colSum; footHtml += `<td class="px-3 py-3 text-center font-mono font-bold text-slate-100 tabular-nums">${colSum.toLocaleString()}</td>`; }); footHtml += `<td class="px-4 py-3 text-right font-mono font-extrabold text-indigo-400 bg-indigo-500/10 border-l border-slate-800 tabular-nums text-sm"> ${grandTotal.toLocaleString()} </td></tr>`; tfoot.innerHTML = footHtml; } function filterFromMatrix(gestion, campana) { const filterGestion = document.getElementById('filterGestion'); const filterCampana = document.getElementById('filterCampana'); if (filterGestion && gestion) filterGestion.value = gestion; if (filterCampana && campana) filterCampana.value = campana; applyFilters(); showToast(`Filtrado por: ${gestion} en ${campana}`, "info"); document.querySelector('#leadsTableBody')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); } function toggleMatrixPanel() { const panel = document.getElementById('matrixPanel'); const button = document.getElementById('matrixToggle'); if (!panel || !button) return; const expanded = panel.classList.toggle('hidden') === false; button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(expanded)); document.getElementById('matrixToggleLabel').textContent = expanded ? 'Ocultar matriz' : 'Mostrar matriz'; } function renderQuickLeads() { const container = document.getElementById('quickLeadsList'); if (!container) return; // Se usa el mismo conjunto que alimenta filtros y tabla. No se consulta // información adicional ni se inventa una prioridad comercial. const rows = [...filteredLeads].sort((a, b) => Number(b.id) - Number(a.id)).slice(0, 4); if (!rows.length) { container.innerHTML = '<div class="quick-leads-empty"><p class="text-sm font-semibold">No hay leads con los filtros seleccionados.</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs">Prueba con otros criterios o limpia los filtros para ver los registros.</p><button type="button" onclick="resetAllFilters()">Limpiar filtros</button></div>'; return; } container.innerHTML = rows.map(lead => { const id = String(lead.id); const origin = getLeadOrigin(lead); const nombre = getField(lead, 'Nombre') || 'Sin nombre'; const campana = getField(lead, 'Campaña') || 'Sin campaña'; const fecha = getField(lead, 'Fecha') || 'Sin fecha'; const gestion = getField(lead, 'GESTION') || 'Sin estado'; return `<div class="quick-lead-row text-xs"> <div class="quick-lead-meta"><button type="button" data-quick-action="view" data-lead-id="${escapeAttr(id)}" data-origin="${origin}" class="font-semibold text-left text-white hover:text-indigo-400 cursor-pointer">${escapeHtml(nombre)}</button><p class="mt-1 text-slate-400">${escapeHtml(fecha)}${origin === 'historico' ? TABLA PRINCIPAL DE LEADS // ------------------------------------------------------------- function renderTable() { const tbody = document.getElementById('leadsTableBody'); if (!tbody) return; if (typeof renderQuickLeads === 'function') renderQuickLeads(); const totalItems = filteredLeads.length; const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalItems / pageSize) || 1; if (currentPage > totalPages) currentPage = totalPages; const startIndex = (currentPage - 1) * pageSize; const endIndex = Math.min(startIndex + pageSize, totalItems); const pageItems = filteredLeads.slice(startIndex, endIndex); if (pageItems.length === 0) { tbody.innerHTML = `<tr> <td colspan="9" class="leads-table-empty"> <i data-lucide="inbox" class="w-8 h-8 mx-auto mb-2 text-slate-600"></i> <p class="text-sm font-semibold">No se encontraron leads con los filtros actuales.</p> <p class="mt-1 text-xs">Prueba con otros criterios o limpia los filtros para ver los registros.</p> <button type="button" onclick="resetAllFilters()">Limpiar filtros</button> </td> </tr>`; updatePaginationUI(0, 0, 0, 1); if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons(); return; } let html = ''; pageItems.forEach((lead, idx) => { const leadId = lead.id || (startIndex + idx + 1); const origin = getLeadOrigin(lead); const originBadge = origin === 'historico' ? fecha = getField(lead, 'Fecha'); const nombre = getField(lead, 'Nombre') || 'Sin Nombre'; const telefono = getField(lead, 'Telefono'); const campana = getField(lead, 'Campaña'); const medio = text-slate-400 tabular-nums">#${leadId}</td> <td class="px-3.5 py-3 text-slate-300"> <div class="font-medium text-white">${escapeHtml(fecha || '-')}</div> <div class="text-[11px] function renderGestionBadge(gestion) { const g = (gestion || '').trim(); const lower = g.toLowerCase(); let colorClasses = "bg-slate-700/50 text-slate-300 border-slate-600/60"; let dotColor = "bg-slate-400"; if (lower.includes('inscrit')) { colorClasses = "bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/30"; dotColor = "bg-emerald-400"; } else if (lower.includes('agendad')) { colorClasses = "bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/30"; dotColor = "bg-amber-400"; } else if (lower.includes('informaci')) { colorClasses = "bg-blue-500/15 text-blue-300 border-blue-500/30"; dotColor = "bg-blue-400"; } else if (lower.includes('no contesta') || lower.includes('colgo') || lower.includes('no procede') || lower.includes('ya no')) { colorClasses = "bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-300 border-rose-500/30"; dotColor = "bg-rose-400"; } else if (lower.includes('vive lejos') || lower.includes('costear') || lower.includes('menor')) { colorClasses = "bg-purple-500/15 text-purple-300 border-purple-500/30"; dotColor = "bg-purple-400"; } return `<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-medium border ${colorClasses}"> <span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${dotColor}"></span> <span class="truncate max-w-[120px]">${escapeHtml(g)}</span> </span>`; } function updatePaginationUI(start, end, total, totalPages) { const infoEl = document.getElementById('paginationInfo'); const controlsEl = document.getElementById('paginationControls'); if (infoEl) { infoEl.innerText = total > 0 ? `Mostrando ${start.toLocaleString()} a ${end.toLocaleString()} de ${total.toLocaleString()} registros` : "Sin registros"; } if (!controlsEl) return; let html = ''; html += `<button aria-label="Página anterior" onclick="goToPage(${currentPage - 1})" ${currentPage <= 1 ? 'disabled class="pagination-arrow px-2 py-1 rounded bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 cursor-not-allowed"' : 'class="pagination-arrow px-2 py-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 cursor-pointer"'}> <i data-lucide="chevron-left" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i> </button>`; const pagesToShow = getPaginationRange(currentPage, totalPages); pagesToShow.forEach(p => { if (p === '...') { html += `<span class="pagination-ellipsis px-2 py-1 text-slate-500">...</span>`; } else { const isActive = p === currentPage; html += `<button onclick="goToPage(${p})" aria-label="Ir a la página ${p}" ${isActive ? 'aria-current="page"' : ''} class="pagination-page-number px-2.5 py-1 rounded text-xs font-mono font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${isActive ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300'}"> ${p} </button>`; } }); html += `<span class="pagination-mobile-label hidden" aria-live="polite">Página ${currentPage} de ${totalPages}</span>`; html += `<button aria-label="Página siguiente" onclick="goToPage(${currentPage + 1})" ${currentPage >= totalPages ? 'disabled class="pagination-arrow px-2 py-1 rounded bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 cursor-not-allowed"' : 'class="pagination-arrow px-2 py-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 cursor-pointer"'}> <i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i> </button>`; controlsEl.innerHTML = html; if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons(); } function getPaginationRange(current, total) { if (total <= 7) return Array.from({ length: total }, (_, i) => i + 1); if (current <= 4) return [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, '...', total]; if (current >= total - 3) return [1, '...', total - 4, total - 3, total - 2, total - 1, total]; return [1, '...', current - 1, current, current + 1, '...', total]; } function goToPage(page) { const totalPages = Math.ceil(filteredLeads.length / pageSize) || 1; if (page >= 1 && page <= totalPages) { currentPage = page; renderTable(); } } function changePageSize(val) { pageSize = parseInt(val, 10) || 20; currentPage = 1; renderTable(); } // ------------------------------------------------------------- // 8. Revisa los datos e inténtalo nuevamente'; } async function handleUpdateLead(event) { event.preventDefault(); if (editLeadSubmitting) return; if (!currentUser || !currentAccess?.activo) return; const leadId = document.getElementById('editLeadId')?.value; if (!leadId) return; if (currentEditOrigin === 'historico') { showToast('El histórico 2025 es de solo lectura', 'warning'); return; } const needsAppointment = editLeadNeedsAppointment(); const scheduledAt = needsAppointment ? appointmentInputISO(document.getElementById('editAppointmentDate')?.value) : null; const appointmentNote = needsAppointment ? note.slice(0, 120) + (note.length > 120 ? '…' : '') : 'Sin nota'}`, '', 'Aceptar creará la cita. Cancelar te permite revisar los datos.' ].join('\n')); } function appointmentCampusFrom(prefix) { return document.querySelector(`input[name="${prefix}Campus"]:checked`)?.value || ''; } function appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt) { return generation === sessionGeneration && viewGeneration === viewLeadGeneration && String(currentViewId) === String(leadId) && currentUser?.id === userId && currentAccess?.updated_at === accessUpdatedAt && currentAccess?.activo; } function setAppointmentMutationPending(id, pending) { const appointmentId = validAppointmentId(id); if (!appointmentId) return; document.querySelectorAll(`[data-appointment-id="${appointmentId}"]`).forEach(control => { if (!('disabled' in control)) return; control.disabled = pending; control.classList.toggle('opacity-50', pending); control.classList.toggle('cursor-not-allowed', pending); }); } async function loadLeadAppointmentAdvisors(lead) { if(!isAdmin)return; } async function loadLeadAppointments(leadId, lead) { const list = document.getElementById('leadAppointmentsList'); if (!list) return; const id = validAppointmentId(leadId); if (!id) return; const generation = sessionGeneration, viewGeneration = viewLeadGeneration, userId = currentUser?.id, accessUpdatedAt = currentAccess?.updated_at; const { data, error } = await fetchPagedResult(() => supabaseClient.from('lead_appointments').select('id, lead_id, scheduled_at, status, advisor_name, advisor_user_id, notes, rescheduled_from_id, resolved_at, created_at, student_name, contact_email, student_age, campus, is_data_dura').eq('lead_id', id)); document.getElementById('leadAppointmentsSection')?.classList.add('hidden'); return; } leadAppointmentsCache = (data || []).sort((a, b) => String(b.scheduled_at).localeCompare(String(a.scheduled_at)) || Number(b.id) - Number(a.id)); if (section && leadAppointmentsCache.length) section.classList.remove('hidden'); updateLeadAppointmentCreateState(lead || findLeadByOrigin('actual', leadId), 'ready'); renderLeadAppointments(); } function renderLeadAppointments() { const list = document.getElementById('leadAppointmentsList'); String(a.student_age) : '-')}</p>` : ''}<p class="text-[11px] text-slate-300">Sede: ${escapeHtml(a.campus || 'Sin sede')}</p>${canEdit ? `<button data-appointment-id="${id}" onclick="editAppointmentDetails(${id})" class="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-600 text-white text-[11px]">Editar datos</button><button data-appointment-id="${id}" onclick="editAppointmentCampus(${id})" class="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-600 text-white text-[11px]">Sede</button>` : ''}${scheduled && canEdit ? `<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5"><button data-appointment-id="${id}" onclick="setLeadAppointmentStatus(${id}, 'ASISTIO')" class="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-emerald-600 text-white text-[11px]">Asistió</button><button data-appointment-id="${id}" onclick="setLeadAppointmentStatus(${id}, 'NO_ASISTIO')" class="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-amber-600 text-white text-[11px]">No asistió</button><button data-appointment-id="${id}" onclick="setLeadAppointmentStatus(${id}, 'CANCELADA')" class="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-rose-600 text-white text-[11px]">Cancelada</button><input data-appointment-id="${id}" id="reschedule-${id}" type="datetime-local" class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded px-2 py-1 text-[11px]"><button data-appointment-id="${id}" onclick="rescheduleLeadAppointment(${id})" class="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-indigo-600 text-white text-[11px]">Reprogramar</button></div>` : ''}${isAdmin ? `<button data-appointment-id="${id}" onclick="deleteLeadAppointment(${id})" class="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-600 text-white text-[11px]">Eliminar cita</button>` : ''}</div>`; }).join('') : '<p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Sin citas registradas.</p>'; } async function createLeadAppointment(event) { event.preventDefault(); const input = document.getElementById('leadAppointmentDate'); const scheduledAt = appointmentInputISO(input?.value); const note = (document.getElementById('leadAppointmentNote')?.value || '').trim(); const submit = document.getElementById('leadAppointmentSubmit'); if (!scheduledAt || note.length > 2000) { showToast('Fecha/hora de Miami no válida o nota demasiado larga', 'warning'); return; } const campus = appointmentCampusFrom('leadAppointment'); if (!campus) { showToast('Selecciona la sede de la cita', 'warning'); return; } const args = { p_lead_id: validAppointmentId(currentViewId), p_scheduled_at: scheduledAt, p_notes: note, p_advisor_user_id: isAdmin ? (document.getElementById('leadAppointmentAdvisor')?.value || null) : null, p_details: appointmentDetailsFrom('leadAppointment'), p_campus: campus }; if (!args.p_lead_id || (isAdmin && !args.p_advisor_user_id)) return; const generation = sessionGeneration, viewGeneration = viewLeadGeneration, leadId = currentViewId, userId = currentUser?.id, accessUpdatedAt = currentAccess?.updated_at, key = `create:${leadId}`; if (pendingAppointmentMutations.has(key)) return; if (!confirmAppointmentCreation('leadAppointment', scheduledAt)) return; const operationToken = Symbol(); pendingAppointmentMutations.set(key, operationToken); if (submit) submit.disabled = true; try { const { error } = await supabaseClient.rpc('create_lead_appointment', args); if (!appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt)) return; if (error) { if (error.message?.includes('lead_not_agendado')) { showToast('Solo los leads agendados pueden tener citas', 'warning'); document.getElementById('leadAppointmentCreate')?.classList.add('hidden'); } else if (String(error.code) === '23505' || String(error.message || '').includes('lead_appointments_one_programmed_per_lead_uidx')) { showToast('Este lead ya tiene una cita programada. Gestiona esa cita antes de crear otra', 'warning'); await loadLeadAppointments(leadId); return; } else showToast('No se pudo crear la cita', 'error'); return; } event.target.reset(); invalidateCitasCache(); await loadLeadAppointments(leadId); if (appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt)) showToast('Cita creada', 'success'); } catch (err) { if (appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt)) { console.warn('No se pudo crear la cita:', err); showToast('No se pudo crear la cita', 'error'); } } finally { if (pendingAppointmentMutations.get(key) === operationToken) pendingAppointmentMutations.delete(key); if (appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt) && document.getElementById('leadAppointmentForm')) { if (submit) submit.disabled = leadAppointmentCreateBlocked; } } } async function editAppointmentDetails(id) { const appointmentId = validAppointmentId(id); if (!appointmentId || pendingAppointmentMutations.has(appointmentId)) return; const current = (leadAppointmentsCache.find(a => validAppointmentId(a.id) === appointmentId)) || (citasCache.find(a => validAppointmentId(a.id) === appointmentId)) || {}; const name = prompt('Nombre del estudiante (dejar vacío para borrar)', current.student_name || ''); if (name === null) return; const email = prompt('Correo de contacto (dejar vacío para borrar)', current.contact_email || ''); if (email === null) return; const age = prompt('Edad del estudiante en años (0-120, dejar vacío para borrar)', current.student_age != null ? String(current.student_age) : ''); if (age === null) return; if (age.trim() && (!/^[0-9]+$/.test(age.trim()) || Number(age) < 0 || Number(age) > 120)) { showToast('Edad no válida', 'warning'); return; } const generation = sessionGeneration, viewGeneration = viewLeadGeneration, leadId = currentViewId, userId = currentUser?.id, accessUpdatedAt = currentAccess?.updated_at; const operationToken = Symbol(); if (!appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt)) return; if (error) { showToast('No se pudieron guardar los datos: ' + (error.message || ''), 'error'); return; } invalidateCitasCache(); await loadLeadAppointments(leadId); if (appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt)) showToast('Datos de la cita actualizados', 'success'); } catch (err) { if (appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt)) { console.warn('No se pudieron guardar los datos:', err); showToast('No se pudieron guardar los datos de la cita', 'error'); } } finally { if (pendingAppointmentMutations.get(appointmentId) === operationToken) pendingAppointmentMutations.delete(appointmentId); if (appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt) && document.querySelector(`[data-appointment-id="${appointmentId}"]`)) setAppointmentMutationPending(appointmentId, false); } } async function editAppointmentCampus(id) { const appointmentId = validAppointmentId(id); if (!appointmentId || pendingAppointmentMutations.has(appointmentId)) return; const current = (leadAppointmentsCache.find(a => validAppointmentId(a.id) === appointmentId)) || (citasCache.find(a => validAppointmentId(a.id) === appointmentId)) || {}; const campus = prompt('Sede de la cita (Doral o Weston, vacío para no cambiar)', current.campus || ''); if (campus === null) return; const v = campus.trim().toUpperCase(); if (!v) return; if (!['DORAL', 'WESTON'].includes(v)) { showToast('Sede no válida (Doral o Weston)', 'warning'); showToast('No se pudo cambiar la sede de la cita', 'error'); } } finally { if (pendingAppointmentMutations.get(appointmentId) === operationToken) pendingAppointmentMutations.delete(appointmentId); if (appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt) && document.querySelector(`[data-appointment-id="${appointmentId}"]`)) setAppointmentMutationPending(appointmentId, false); } } async function rescheduleLeadAppointment(id) { const appointmentId = validAppointmentId(id); const scheduledAt = appointmentInputISO(document.getElementById(`reschedule-${appointmentId}`)?.value); if (!appointmentId || !scheduledAt || pendingAppointmentMutations.has(appointmentId)) { showToast('Fecha y hora de Miami no válidas', 'warning'); return; } const generation = sessionGeneration, viewGeneration = viewLeadGeneration, leadId = currentViewId, userId = currentUser?.id, accessUpdatedAt = currentAccess?.updated_at; const operationToken = Symbol(); pendingAppointmentMutations.set(appointmentId, operationToken); setAppointmentMutationPending(appointmentId, true); try { const { error } = await supabaseClient.rpc('reschedule_lead_appointment', { p_id: appointmentId, p_scheduled_at: scheduledAt, p_notes: '' }); if (!appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt)) return; if (error) { showToast('No se pudo reprogramar la cita', 'error'); return; } invalidateCitasCache(); await loadLeadAppointments(leadId); if (appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt)) showToast('Cita reprogramada', 'success'); } catch (err) { if (appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt)) { console.warn('No se pudo reprogramar la cita:', err); showToast('No se pudo reprogramar la cita', 'error'); } } finally { if (pendingAppointmentMutations.get(appointmentId) === operationToken) pendingAppointmentMutations.delete(appointmentId); if (appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt) && document.querySelector(`[data-appointment-id="${appointmentId}"]`)) setAppointmentMutationPending(appointmentId, false); } } // T2: estado del formulario de creacion de citas de la ficha del lead. // True por defecto: si el script falla antes de decidir, el formulario sigue cerrado. // Fail-closed en las tres fases While loading: el formulario nunca se ofrece // antes de saber si el lead ya tiene una cita PROGRAMADA. // phase: 'loading' | 'error' | 'ready' let leadAppointmentCreateBlocked = true; function updateLeadAppointmentCreateState(lead, phase) { const createWrap = document.getElementById('leadAppointmentCreate'); const notice = document.getElementById('leadAppointmentNotice'); if (!createWrap || !notice) return; const submit = createWrap.querySelector?.('[data-appointment-create]') || null; const block = () => { leadAppointmentCreateBlocked = true; setBlockControls(createWrap, true); if (submit) submit.disabled = true; }; const unblock = () => { leadAppointmentCreateBlocked = false; setBlockControls(createWrap, false); if (submit) submit.disabled = false; }; const showNotice = message => { notice.textContent = message; notice.classList.remove('hidden'); notice.classList.add('text-[11px]', 'text-slate-500'); }; const hideNotice = () => { notice.classList.add('hidden'); notice.classList.remove('text-[11px]', 'text-slate-500'); }; if (phase === 'loading') { createWrap.classList.remove('hidden'); block(); showNotice('Comprobando si ya hay una cita programada…'); return; } if (phase === 'error') { createWrap.classList.add('hidden'); block(); showNotice('No se pudo verificar si hay una cita programada; no se pueden crear citas hasta recargar.'); return; } if (!isAgendadoGestion(getField(lead || {}, 'GESTION'))) { createWrap.classList.add('hidden'); block(); showNotice('El lead no está agendado; no se pueden crear nuevas citas.'); return; } if (leadAppointmentsCache.some(a => String(a.status) === 'PROGRAMADA')) { createWrap.classList.remove('hidden'); block(); showNotice('Este lead ya tiene una cita programada. Gestiona esa cita (Asistió / No asistió / Cancelar / Reprogramar) antes de crear otra.'); return; } createWrap.classList.remove('hidden'); unblock(); hideNotice(); } // T3: borrado definitivo de UNA cita, solo administrador. // La RPC valida is_admin_user() en el servidor: ocultar el boton es cortesia, // la garantia real esta en delete_lead_appointment. async function deleteLeadAppointment(id) { const appointmentId = validAppointmentId(id); if (!appointmentId || pendingAppointmentMutations.has(appointmentId)) return; if (!isAdmin) { showToast('Solo el administrador puede eliminar citas', 'error'); return; } const current = (leadAppointmentsCache.find(a => validAppointmentId(a.id) === appointmentId)) || (citasCache.find(a => validAppointmentId(a.id) === appointmentId)) || null; if (!current) { showToast('No se encontró la cita a eliminar', 'error'); return; } const leadLabel = getField(current.lead || {}, 'Nombre') || `lead #${current.lead_id}`; const confirmed = confirm([ 'Eliminar esta cita de forma definitiva?', `Lead: ${leadLabel}`, `Fecha y hora: ${appointmentLocal(current.scheduled_at)}`, `Estado: ${String(current.status || '-')}`, `Asesor: ${current.advisor_name || 'sin asesor'}`, `Sede: ${current.campus || 'sin sede'}`, '', 'Solo se borra esta cita y su historial de eventos.', 'El lead, sus notas, sus gestiones y sus demas citas se conservan.', 'Esta accion no se puede deshacer.' ].join('\n')); if (!confirmed) return; const generation = sessionGeneration, viewGeneration = viewLeadGeneration, leadId = currentViewId, userId = currentUser?.id, accessUpdatedAt = currentAccess?.updated_at; const operationToken = Symbol(); pendingAppointmentMutations.set(appointmentId, operationToken); setAppointmentMutationPending(appointmentId, true); try { const { error } = await supabaseClient.rpc('delete_lead_appointment', { p_id: appointmentId }); if (!appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt)) return; if (error) { const denied = error.message?.includes('admin_required'); showToast(denied ? 'Solo el administrador puede eliminar citas' : 'No se pudo eliminar la cita: ' + (error.message || ''), 'error'); return; } invalidateCitasCache(); if (leadId) await loadLeadAppointments(leadId); if (appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt)) showToast('Cita eliminada', 'success'); } catch (err) { if (appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt)) { console.warn('No se pudo eliminar la cita:', err); showToast('No se pudo eliminar la cita', 'error'); } } finally { if (pendingAppointmentMutations.get(appointmentId) === operationToken) pendingAppointmentMutations.delete(appointmentId); if (appointmentSessionValid(generation, viewGeneration, leadId, userId, accessUpdatedAt) && document.querySelector(`[data-appointment-id="${appointmentId}"]`)) setAppointmentMutationPending(appointmentId, false); } } // ------------------------------------------------------------- // Notas de equipo (lead_notes): append-only vía RPC; editar/borrar solo admin. // ------------------------------------------------------------- async function loadLeadNotes(leadId) { const list = document.getElementById('leadNotesList'); if (!list) return; list.innerHTML = '<p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500" role="status">Cargando notas…</p>'; // T4: patron sessionGeneration en exito y error (ficha+sesion+usuario). const generation = sessionGeneration, viewGeneration = viewLeadGeneration, userId = currentUser?.id, accessUpdatedAt = currentAccess?.updated_at; const { data, error } = await fetchPagedResult(() => supabaseClient .from('lead_notes') .select('id, lead_id, author_name, note, created_at, updated_at') .eq('lead_id', leadId)); // La ficha o la sesion pudo cambiarse mientras esperábamos: descarta la // respuesta obsoleta sin pintar ni limpiar datos ajenos. if (generation !== sessionGeneration || viewGeneration !== viewLeadGeneration || currentUser?.id !== userId || currentAccess?.updated_at !== accessUpdatedAt || String(currentViewId) !== String(leadId)) return; if (error) { list.innerHTML = '<div role="alert" class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-rose-400"><p>No se pudieron cargar las notas.</p><button type="button" onclick="loadLeadNotes(currentViewId)" class="min-h-11 rounded-lg border border-rose-400 px-3 py-2 font-semibold hover:bg-rose-400/10 focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-rose-300">Reintentar</button></div>'; return; } if (!data || data.length === 0) { list.innerHTML = '<p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Sin notas registradas.</p>'; leadNotesCache = []; return; } leadNotesCache = data.sort((a, b) => String(b.created_at).localeCompare(String(a.created_at)) || Number(b.id) - Number(a.id)); renderLeadNotes(); } async function loadLeadGestiones(leadId) { const section = document.getElementById('leadGestionesSection'); const list = document.getElementById('leadGestionesList'); if (!section || !list) return; // T4: patron sessionGeneration en exito y error (ficha+sesion+usuario). const generation = sessionGeneration, viewGeneration = viewLeadGeneration, userId = currentUser?.id, accessUpdatedAt = currentAccess?.updated_at; const { data, error } = await fetchPagedResult(() => supabaseClient .from('lead_gestiones') .select('id, fecha_gestion, gestion_anterior, gestion_nueva, canal, is_data_dura, autor_name, created_at') .eq('lead_id', leadId)); if (generation !== sessionGeneration || viewGeneration !== viewLeadGeneration || currentUser?.id !== userId || currentAccess?.updated_at !== accessUpdatedAt || String(currentViewId) !== String(leadId)) return; if (error) { console.warn('No se pudo cargar el historial de gestiones:', error); return; } leadGestionesCache = (data || []).sort((a, b) => String(a.fecha_gestion).localeCompare(String(b.fecha_gestion)) || String(a.created_at).localeCompare(String(b.created_at)) || Number(a.id) - Number(b.id)); section.classList.remove('hidden'); renderLeadGestiones(); } function renderLeadGestiones() { const list = document.getElementById('leadGestionesList'); if (!list) return; if (!leadGestionesCache.length) { list.innerHTML = '<p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Sin cambios registrados todavía.</p>'; return; } list.innerHTML = leadGestionesCache.map(g => { const fecha = String(g.fecha_gestion || '').split('-').reverse().join('/') || '-'; const anterior = String(g.gestion_anterior || '').trim(); const estados = anterior ? `${renderGestionBadge(anterior)} <span class="text-slate-500 mx-1">→</span> ${renderGestionBadge(g.gestion_nueva || '-')}` : renderGestionBadge(g.gestion_nueva || '-'); return `<div class="border-b border-slate-800 last:border-0 pb-2 last:pb-0 space-y-1"> <div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px]"><span class="text-slate-300 font-medium">${escapeHtml(fecha)}</span>${estados}</div> <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">${escapeHtml((g.is_data_dura === true && String(g.canal||'').trim().toLowerCase() === 'llamada') ? 'Llamada Data Dura' : (String(g.canal || '') === 'Llamada y WhatsApp' ? 'Llamada WhatsApp' : (g.canal || '-')))} · ${escapeHtml(g.autor_name || 'Agente')}</div> </div>`; }).join(''); } // Render del timeline desde cache. editingNoteId (solo admin) muestra el textarea inline. function renderLeadNotes() { const list = document.getElementById('leadNotesList'); if (!list) return; if (!leadNotesCache.length) { list.innerHTML = '<p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Sin notas registradas.</p>'; return; } list.innerHTML = leadNotesCache.map(n => { const fecha = n.created_at ? new Date(n.created_at).toLocaleString('es-ES', { dateStyle: 'short', timeStyle: 'short' }) : ''; const edited = n.updated_at && n.updated_at !== n.created_at ? ' · editada' : ''; const editing = isAdmin && editingNoteId === Number(n.id); const actions = editing ? ` <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0"> <button onclick="saveLeadNoteInline(${Number(n.id)})" class="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white text-[11px] font-semibold cursor-pointer transition-colors">Guardar</button> <button onclick="cancelLeadNoteEdit()" class="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 text-[11px] font-medium cursor-pointer transition-colors border border-slate-700">Cancelar</button> </div>` : isAdmin ? ` <div class="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0"> <button onclick="editLeadNote(${Number(n.id)})" title="Editar nota" class="p-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-indigo-500/20 text-slate-400 hover:text-indigo-300 transition-colors cursor-pointer"><i data-lucide="pencil" class="w-3 h-3"></i></button> <button onclick="deleteLeadNote(${Number(n.id)})" title="Borrar nota" class="p-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-rose-500/20 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-300 transition-colors cursor-pointer"><i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-3 h-3"></i></button> </div>` : ''; const body = editing ? `<textarea id="leadNoteEditInput" rows="3" maxlength="2000" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-indigo-500 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-100 focus:outline-none resize-y">${escapeHtml(n.note)}</textarea>` : `<p class="text-xs text-slate-300 whitespace-pre-line leading-relaxed break-words">${escapeHtml(n.note)}</p>`; return `<div data-note-id="${Number(n.id)}" class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 border-b border-slate-800 last:border-0 pb-2 last:pb-0"> <div class="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-0.5"> <div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px]"> <span class="text-indigo-300 font-semibold">${escapeHtml(n.author_name || 'Agente')}</span> <span class="text-slate-500">${escapeHtml(fecha)}${escapeHtml(edited)}</span> </div> ${body} </div> ${actions} </div>`; }).join(''); if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons(); if (editingNoteId !== null) { const ta = document.getElementById('leadNoteEditInput'); if (ta) { ta.focus(); ta.setSelectionRange(ta.value.length, ta.value.length); } } } async function addLeadNote() { const input = document.getElementById('leadNoteInput'); const btn = document.getElementById('leadNoteSubmit'); const leadId = currentViewId; const note = (input?.value || '').trim(); if (!note) { showToast('La nota no puede estar vacía', 'warning'); return; } if (btn) btn.disabled = true; try { const { error } = await supabaseClient.rpc('create_lead_note', { p_lead_id: leadId, p_note: note }); if (error) { showToast('No se pudo guardar la nota', 'error'); return; } if (input) input.value = ''; await loadLeadNotes(leadId); showToast('Nota añadida', 'success'); } finally { if (btn) btn.disabled = false; } } // Edición inline: convierte la nota en textarea en su sitio (sin prompt()). function editLeadNote(noteId) { if (!isAdmin) return; editingNoteId = Number(noteId); renderLeadNotes(); } function cancelLeadNoteEdit() { editingNoteId = null; renderLeadNotes(); } async function saveLeadNoteInline(noteId) { if (!isAdmin) return; const ta = document.getElementById('leadNoteEditInput'); const trimmed = (ta?.value || '').trim(); if (!trimmed) { showToast('La nota no puede estar vacía', 'warning'); return; } const { error } = await supabaseClient.rpc('update_lead_note', { p_id: noteId, p_note: trimmed }); if (error) { showToast('No se pudo editar la nota', 'error'); return; } editingNoteId = null;
